The Euclidean projection onto box+sum is `P_i = clip(x_i + tau,0,1)` for some tau. Because Lagrangian for min ||h-x||^2 s.t. 0≤h≤1, sum=target -> h_i=clip(x_i+tau,0,1). Yes! Use this. Bisection tau. This is exact and O(n*iterations). Fast. Good.
```
def project_feasible(x,n):
    target=n/2
    # if target outside [0,n] not
    lo=-1.0; hi=1.0 # x in [0,1], tau enough
    # f(tau)=sum(clip(x+tau,0,1))-target, monotone
    for 60:
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       val=np.sum(np.clip(x+mid,0,1))
       if val>target: hi=mid else lo=mid
    tau=(lo+hi)/2
    h=np.clip(x+tau,0,1)
    # correct residual due to floating: interior adjust
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>0:
       free=np.where((h>1e-15)&(h<1-1e-15))[0]
       if len(free)>0:
          h[free]+=diff/len(free)
          # if goes out, loop clip & bisect again maybe
          h=np.clip(h,0,1)
          # residual small; distribute among interior repeatedly
    return h
```
Bisection f with monotonic. If there are plateaus (many clip) maybe tau interval; any τ works. After exact correction, small rounding. ```
def exact_pair_polish(h, c_raw, n, nfft, deadline, max_steps=5000, nfft_grad):
  h=project_feasible; c_raw=corr_raw(h) maybe
  best_c = c_raw.max()*dx
  steps_no_improve=0
  while time<deadline:
    c_max = c_raw.max()
    best_c = c_max*dx
    # active tol
    active_tol = max(1e-8, 5*dx) maybe 0.002? For 1000 dx=0.002; 5dx=0.01 too broad. Use 0.001? We want near max. Use active_tol = max(1e-6, 0.001/dx? no c scale). Maybe active_tol = max(1e-6, 3*dx). dx=0.002 =>0.006. If gradient broad too. Use 2*dx.
    active = c_raw >= c_max - max(1e-8, 2*dx)
    if active count < 1: active mid.
    w = zeros; w[active]=1/active.sum()
    # optional include weights by softmax top if too many? uniform okay.
    g = gradient_from_weights(w,h,n,nfft)
    donors = indices g>1e-12 & h>1e-10; receivers g<-1e-12 & h<1-1e-10
    sort by abs(g)*room? top K.
    best_pair = None; best_F = c_max - 1e-12; best_eps=0
    K=6 or 8.
    For i in donors[:K]: D_i = D_var(i)
      For j in receivers[:K]: if i==j skip
        d = D_j - D_i
        eps_bound = min(h[i], 1-h[j])
        if eps_bound <= 1e-12 skip
        # quick check derivative at 0? F upper? maybe sample 16 points to find rough min, then refine.
        (e, fval_raw) = minimize_pair_eps(c_raw,d,eps_bound,n, tol)

`D_var(h,x,n)`:
```
def D_var(h, x, n):
    D = np.zeros(2*n-1, dtype=float)
    # first contributions
    D[n-1-x:2*n-1-x] += one_minus_h  # but one_minus_h global? compute inside
    D[x:n+x] += -h[::-1]
    return D
```

Let's examine mathematical correctness of gradient formula with weights normalized over raw or c. In smooth objective `val = smooth_max(c)`, where c=raw*dx. We compute weights w from c. w sum 1. d val/d h = ∑ w * dx * d raw/dh = dx * grad_raw_w. Our `gradient_from_weights` returns ∑ w d raw/dh if w sum 1. Multiply by dx. Good.

Potential issue with `project_feasible` tau bisection: If x already in [0,1], target n/2, there may be many tau if plateaus. Bisection 70 iterations enough. Implementation details:
```
def project_feasible(x,n):
    x = np.asarray(x,dtype=float)
    target = n/2.0
    # If exact? Use bisection
    lo=-1.0; hi=1.0
    # f(t)=sum(clip(x+t,0,1))-target
    # If f(1)<target (sum max <target), return all? not possible since target avg0.5 and x in [0,1] maybe sum min but if all x=0, target n/2, f(1)=n? >target. f(-1)=0<target.
    for _ in range(80):
      mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
      val=np.sum(np.clip(x+mid,0.0,1.0))
      if val > target: hi=mid
      else: lo=mid
    tau=(lo+hi)*0.5
    h=np.clip(x+tau,0.0,1.0)
    # exact correction
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if diff != 0:
      for _ in range(100):
        free=np.where((h>1e-14)&(h<1-1e-14))[0]
        if len(free)==0: break
        # To avoid going out, distribute max possible evenly? uniform may over/under if diff large but residual tiny.
        step=diff/len(free)
        # if any would go out, use small version: scale
        if step >0:
           maxadd = 1.0 - h[free]
           if maxadd.min() < step:
               # allocate to those with maxadd < step maybe loop
               h[free] += np.minimum(step, maxadd)
        else:
           maxsub = h[free]
           if maxsub.min() < -step:
               h[free] += np.maximum(step, -maxsub)  # careful
        h=np.clip(h,0,1); diff=target-h.sum(); if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
    return h
```
Since tau projection should make sum target within 1e-9; correction small. Simpler: after tau, diff maybe 1e-12; adjust one free element by diff if possible:
```
free = np.where((h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12))[0]
if diff != 0 and len(free)>0:
   h[free[0]] += diff
   if not (0<=h[free[0]]<=1) or h.sum()... distribute.
```
But if diff larger due to plateau? tau bisection ensures sum within float. Use iterative.

def upsample_h(h, n):
    if len(h)==n: return project(h)
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,len(h)-1)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n-1)
    if len(h)==1: ...
    new=np.interp(x_new,x_old,h)
    # ensure endpoint? maybe also replicate last
    return project(new,n)
```

Exact evaluation:
```
def exact_c5(h,n):
  dx=2.0/n
  c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')*dx
  return float(c.max())

def weights_from_c(c, alpha):
  m = c.max()
  if not np.isfinite(m): return np.zeros, m
  sh = alpha*(c-m)
  shmax = sh.max()
  if not np.isfinite(shmax) or shmax < -745: # all underflow except max?
      w=np.zeros(len(c)); w[np.argmax(c)] = 1.0; return w,m
  w=np.exp(sh-shmax); s=w.sum()
  if s==0...: w=max
  w/=s
  val = m + (shmax + np.log(s))/alpha
  return w,val
```

def pair_min_eval(c_raw, d, e_bound, q_idx, q_val, grid_num=25, refine_num=17):
    m0 = c_raw.max()
    best_e = 0.0; best_f = m0
    tmp = np.empty_like(c_raw)
    for e in np.linspace(0.0, e_bound, grid_num):
        e=float(e)
        if e == 0.0:
           val = m0
        else:
           np.multiply(d, e, out=tmp)
           tmp += c_raw
           # add q
           for idx,val in zip(q_idx,q_val): tmp[idx] += e*e*val
           val = tmp.max()
        if val < best_f - 1e-15:
            best_f=val; best_e=e
    if 0 < best_e < e_bound:
       step = e_bound/(grid_num-1)
       lo=max(0.0,best_e-step); hi=min(e_bound,best_e+step)
       for e in np.linspace(lo,hi,refine_num): ...
    return best_e,best_f
```

  target=n//2

     idx = int((s + k*0.61803398875)%1 * n)
     if h[idx]==0: h[idx]=1
  # adjust target

1. `project_feasible`.
```
def project_feasible(x, n):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    target = 0.5 * n
    lo = -1.0
    hi = 1.0
    # if target outside [0,n], not
    for _ in range(70):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        val = np.sum(np.clip(x + mid, 0.0, 1.0))
        if val > target:
            hi = mid
        else:
            lo = mid
    tau = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    h = np.clip(x + tau, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 0.0:
        for _ in range(100):
            if abs(diff) < 1e-15:
                break
            free = np.where((h > 1e-13) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-13))[0]
            if free.size == 0:
                # adjust all slightly? If all 0/1 and sum infeasible, target n/2 means half should be 1; pick floor.
                # This should not happen.
                break
            step = diff / float(free.size)
            # to avoid bound exceed, apply saturating
            if diff > 0:
                add = np.minimum(max(step, 0.0), 1.0 - h[free])
            else:
                add = np.maximum(min(step, 0.0), -h[free])
            h[free] += add
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
            diff = target - h.sum()
    # If still diff and free, adjust one or distribute
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-15:
        free = np.where((h > 1e-14) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-14))[0]
        if free.size > 0:
            # distribute by proportional to room
            if diff > 0:
                room = 1.0 - h[free]
                if room.sum() > 0:
                    h[free] += diff * (room / room.sum())
            else:
                room = h[free]
                if room.sum() > 0:
                    h[free] += diff * (room / room.sum())
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h
```
This correction may still leave tiny diff. Could loop once more.
Given target n/2, if x all 0/1 and sum off by large? But L-BFGS with penalty likely fractional. If initial parent already feasible. Good.
Maybe simpler: after bisection, residual <1e-12; just adjust one interior:
```
if diff !=0:
 free=...
 if free.size: 
   h[free[0]] += diff
   if infeasible distribute...
```
But if diff 1e-12, one interior fine. Let's keep robust.

2. `exact_c5`.
```
def exact_c5(h,n):
    dx = 2.0/n
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full') * dx
    return float(np.max(c))
```

3. `upsample_h`.
```
def upsample_h(h,n):
    h = np.asarray(h,float)
    m=len(h)
    if m==n: return project_feasible(h,n)
    if m==1: return project_feasible(np.full(n,h[0]),n)
    old = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m-1)
    new = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n-1)
    out = np.interp(new, old, h)
    return project_feasible(out,n)
```
Need if m==0? not.

4. `fft_plan(n)` returns nfft,rOnes? Top-level.
```
def prep_fft(n):
    need = 3*n - 2
    nfft = 1
    while nfft < need: nfft <<= 1
    ones = np.zeros(nfft)
    ones[:n]=1.0
    rOnes=np.fft.rfft(ones)
    return nfft, rOnes
```

5. `raw_corr_fft(h,n,nfft,rOnes)`:
```
def raw_corr_fft(h,n,nfft,rOnes):
    rH = np.fft.rfft(h,nfft)
    rB = rOnes - rH
    rHr = np.fft.rfft(h[::-1], nfft)
    raw = np.fft.irfft(rHr * rB, nfft)
    raw = raw[:2*n-1]
    # small negative clip
    # raw may be -1e-15; clip to 0 only if min > -1e-9?
    if raw.size:
       # Use np.maximum(raw,0.0) maybe changes tiny positive? okay.
       np.maximum(raw,0.0,out=raw)
    return raw
```
This clips all negatives to 0, okay since true nonnegative. Could overstate if FFT negative significant due to error? no.

6. `weights_value(c, alpha)`:
```
def weights_value(c, alpha):
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
       w=np.zeros_like(c); w[0]=1.0; return w,m
    sh = alpha * (c - m)
    shmax = float(np.max(sh))
    if not np.isfinite(shmax):
       # if alpha=0? not; set max
       w=np.zeros_like(c); idx=int(np.argmax(c)); w[idx]=1.0; return w,m
    if shmax < -745.0:
       w=np.zeros_like(c); idx=int(np.argmax(c)); w[idx]=1.0; return w,m
    w = np.exp(sh - shmax)
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       w = np.zeros_like(c); idx=int(np.argmax(c)); w[idx]=1.0; return w,m
    w /= s
    val = m + (shmax + np.log(s)) / alpha
    return w, val
```
For alpha small? If alpha=0 would divide; not used.

7. `smooth_value` and `smooth_grad` with args:
```
def smooth_value(x,n,alpha,nfft,rOnes):
   raw=raw_corr_fft(x,n,nfft,rOnes)
   c=raw*(2.0/n)
   _, val=weights_value(c,alpha)
   return val

def smooth_grad(x,n,alpha,nfft,rOnes):
   raw=raw_corr_fft(x,n,nfft,rOnes)
   c=raw*(2.0/n)
   w,_=weights_value(c,alpha)
   g = gradient_from_weights(x,w,n,nfft)
   return g*(2.0/n)
```
Duplicate raw. Good.

8. `gradient_from_weights(h,w,n,nfft,rH=None)`:
```
def gradient_from_weights(h,w,n,nfft,rH=None):
    if rH is None:
        rH = np.fft.rfft(h, nfft)
    rW = np.fft.rfft(w, nfft)
    rWr = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], nfft)
    conv1 = np.fft.irfft(rH * rWr, nfft)
    conv2 = np.fft.irfft(rH * rW, nfft)
    T = conv1[n-1:2*n-1]
    U = conv2[n-1:2*n-1]
    P = np.empty(2*n)
    P[0]=0.0
    P[1:]=np.cumsum(w) # length 2n-1, so P length 2n
    j = np.arange(n)
    S = P[2*n-1 - j] - P[n-1 - j]
    return S - T - U
```
Check P length: w length 2n-1, cumsum length 2n-1. P[1:]=cumsum length 2n-1, P size 2n okay. S indices: for j=n-1, 2n-1-(n-1)=n, P[n] exists; for j=0, 2n-1, P[2n-1] exists. Good.
`np.arange(n)` creates int. Good.

Could use `nfft` have to be >= 3n-2; if not, conv length wrap. We'll use that.

9. Penalties for L-BFGS:
```
def smooth_value_pen(x,n,alpha,nfft,rOnes,mu,lam):
    val = smooth_value(...)
    s = x.sum() - 0.5*n
    val += 0.5*mu*s*s + lam*s
    return val

def smooth_grad_pen(x,n,alpha,nfft,rOnes,mu,lam):
    g = smooth_grad(...)
    s = x.sum() - 0.5*n
    g = g + (mu*s + lam) * 1.0  # broadcasting scalar to array; g + scalar works
    return g
```
However `g + (mu*s+lam) * 1.0` allocates; could `g += ...` but g from smooth_grad new, okay. Return.

10. `run_lbfgs_aug`:
```
def run_lbfgs_aug(h0,n,deadline,alphas,mu_init,maxit,nfft,rOnes):
    dx=2.0/n
    h=project_feasible(h0,n)
    c0=raw_corr_fft(h,n,nfft,rOnes).max()*dx
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c0
    lam=0.0; mu=mu_init
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)] for? Need list: [(0.0,1.0)]*n
    for alpha in alphas:
       if time.time() > deadline - 5: break
       # maybe adjust maxit based on n: already passed
       try:
         res = minimize(smooth_value_pen, h, jac=smooth_grad_pen, args=(n,alpha,nfft,rOnes,mu,lam), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxit,'ftol':1e-20,'gtol':1e-12,'maxfun':maxit*10})
         x_new = res.x
         if not np.all(np.isfinite(x_new)): x_new=h
       except Exception:
         x_new=h
       h_new=project_feasible(x_new,n)
       raw_new=raw_corr_fft(h_new,n,nfft,rOnes)
       c_new=raw_new.max()*dx
       # update best
       if c_new < best_c - 1e-14:
          best_c=c_new; best_h=h_new.copy()
       # decide current for next
       # Use h_new if c_new < c0 + 0.02 or res.success? We can just h=h_new (to continue around alpha) but if much worse, keep best? To avoid noise, if c_new < best_c + 0.01: h=h_new else h=best_h
       if c_new < best_c + 0.005:
           h=h_new
       else:
           h=best_h
       diff = h.sum() - 0.5*n
       lam = lam - mu*diff
       if lam > 1e3: lam=1e3
       if lam < -1e3: lam=-1e3
       if abs(diff) > 1e-4:
           mu = min(1e4, mu*2.0)
       elif abs(diff) < 1e-6:
           mu = max(mu_init, mu*0.5)
    # final maybe project
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,n)
    return best_h, float(raw_corr_fft(best_h,n,nfft,rOnes).max()*dx)
```
Using `raw_corr_fft` for c, not exact. okay.
`bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n` note tuple repeated. Good.
Potential issue: If `mu` grows, penalty gradient huge and L-BFGS may step large. But target.

Could add `if res.success` and maybe run each alpha twice? Not.

12. `D_var`:
```
def D_var(h,x,n):
    D=np.zeros(2*n-1,dtype=float)
    D[n-1-x:2*n-1-x] += (1.0 - h)
    D[x:n+x] += -h[::-1]
    return D
```
Check slice end for first: 2*n-1-x exclusive. For x=0, start n-1, end 2n-1 (last index 2n-2) length n. For x=n-1, start 0, end n length n. Good.
Second: x:n+x length n. For x=n-1, start n-1,end 2n-1 length n. Values -h[::-1] (length n). Good.

13. `pair_min`:
```
def pair_min(c_raw,d,eps_bound,q_idx,q_val,grid_num=25,refine_num=13):
    m0 = float(c_raw.max())
    best_e=0.0; best_f=m0
    tmp=np.empty_like(c_raw)
    # evaluate grid
    for k in range(grid_num):
       e = eps_bound * k / (grid_num-1)
       if k==0: val=m0
       else:
          np.multiply(d, e, out=tmp)
          tmp += c_raw
          e2 = e*e
          for ii in range(q_idx.size):
             tmp[q_idx[ii]] += e2 * q_val[ii]
          val = float(tmp.max())
       if val < best_f - 1e-15:
          best_f=val; best_e=e
    if 0.0 < best_e < eps_bound:
       step = eps_bound/(grid_num-1)
       lo = best_e - step; if lo<0: lo=0
       hi = best_e + step; if hi>eps: hi=eps
       # refine uniform
       for k in range(refine_num):
          e = lo + (hi-lo)*k/(refine_num-1)
          ... evaluate, update
    return best_e,best_f
```
Potential issue: `tmp += c_raw` leaves tmp from previous scaled d; after q adjustment, next loop `np.multiply(d,e,out=tmp)` overwrites fully, then `tmp += c_raw`. Good. q adjustments not reset because multiply overwrites. Good.
If `grid_num=25`, includes e=0 and bound. If eps_bound very small, okay.
Use `grid_num=25, refine_num=15`. If eps_bound small? no issue.

Could evaluate endpoint bound first; if best not 0. Good.

14. `exact_pair_polish`:
Important to return best_h from initial and c. If starting h has c but we update c_raw; if h changes and c_raw no longer exact, best still. For final, in run we exact eval.
```
def exact_pair_polish(h0,n,deadline,nfft,rOnes,grid_num=25,refine_num=15):
    dx=2.0/n
    h=project_feasible(h0,n)
    c_raw=raw_corr_fft(h,n,nfft,rOnes)
    best_c = float(c_raw.max()*dx)
    best_h=h.copy()
    no_improve=0
    steps=0
    K = 6 if n <= 1200 else 5
    top_k = 8 if n <= 1200 else 6
    while time.time() < deadline - 1.0:
       cmax_raw = float(c_raw.max())
       cur_c = cmax_raw*dx
       if cur_c < best_c - 1e-14:
           best_c=cur_c; best_h=h.copy()
       # gradient efficient: rH
       rH = np.fft.rfft(h, nfft)
       score_d=np.zeros(n)
       score_r=np.zeros(n)
       # mode broad
       active_tol_c = max(1e-7, 2.0*dx)
       active = c_raw >= cmax_raw - active_tol_c/dx
       if not active.any(): active[int(np.argmax(c_raw))]=True
       w=np.zeros_like(c_raw); val=1.0/active.sum(); w[active]=val
       g=gradient_from_weights(h,w,n,nfft,rH)
       dsc = g * np.minimum(h,1.0)
       dsc[g<=1e-10] = -1e30
       rsc = -g * np.minimum(1.0-h,1.0)
       rsc[g>=-1e-10] = -1e30
       score_d=np.maximum(score_d,dsc)
       score_r=np.maximum(score_r,rsc)
       # if no_improve > 5, top sharp
       if no_improve > 4:
          idx = np.argpartition(c_raw, -top_k)[-top_k:]
          w2=np.zeros_like(c_raw); w2[idx]=1.0/top_k
          g2=gradient_from_weights(h,w2,n,nfft,rH)
          dsc2 = g2*np.minimum(h,1.0); dsc2[g2<=1e-10]=-1e30
          rsc2 = -g2*np.minimum(1.0-h,1.0); rsc2[g2>=-1e-10]=-1e30
          score_d=np.maximum(score_d,dsc2); score_r=np.maximum(score_r,rsc2)
       donors = np.argsort(-score_d)[:K]
       receivers = np.argsort(-score_r)[:K]
       # filter valid
       dop = []; rop=[]
       for i in donors: 
          ii=int(i)
          if h[ii] > 1e-10 and score_d[ii] > 1e-20: dop.append(ii)
       for j in receivers:
          jj=int(j)
          if h[jj] < 1.0 - 1e-10 and score_r[jj] > 1e-20: rop.append(jj)
       if len(dop)==0 or len(rop)==0:
           no_improve += 1
           if no_improve > 20: break
           continue
       # precompute Dcache
       Dcache = {}
       for ii in dop: Dcache[ii]=D_var(h,ii,n)
       for jj in rop: Dcache[jj]=D_var(h,jj,n)
       best_pair=None; best_f=cmax_raw
       for ii in dop:
           eps_max_i = h[ii]
           Di = Dcache[ii]
           for jj in rop:
               if ii == jj: continue
               eps_bound = min(eps_max_i, 1.0 - h[jj])
               if eps_bound <= 1e-12: continue
               Djj = Dcache[jj]
               d = Djj - Di
               q_idx = np.array([n-1, n-1 + jj - ii, n-1 + ii - jj], dtype=int)
               q_val = np.array([-2.0,1.0,1.0])
               # ensure unique? if jj==ii skip; if cross p == mid? only if ii==jj; okay.
               e, f = pair_min(c_raw, d, eps_bound, q_idx, q_val, grid_num, refine_num)
               if f < best_f - 1e-13:
                   best_f=f; best_pair=(ii,jj,e)
       if best_pair is None:
           no_improve += 1
           if no_improve > 25:
               break
           # maybe random small perturbation: choose first donors/receivers with small e=0.05*epsbound; evaluate if not increase more than 1e-6? skip
           continue
       ii,jj,e = best_pair
       if e <= 1e-14:
           no_improve += 1; continue
       Dcache... Need d/q to update c_raw. We lost d; recompute D? We can recompute from Dcache (still available) or store. Dcache available before; use.
       Djj = Dcache[jj]; Di=Dcache[ii]; d = Djj - Di
       q_idx... q_val...
       np.multiply(d,e,out=buf)? But no buf.
       c_raw = c_raw + e*d
       c_raw[q_idx[0]] += e*e*q_val[0] ... use loop.
       h[ii] -= e; h[jj] += e
       # clip tiny
       if h[ii] < 0: h[ii]=0; elif >1: =1; same jj. (shouldn't)
       # After update, sum may drift tiny. If abs(sum-target)>1e-10, project and recompute
       steps +=1
       if steps % 5 == 0:
           c_raw = raw_corr_fft(h,n,nfft,rOnes)
           rH = None
           cur_c = float(c_raw.max()*dx)
           if cur_c < best_c - 1e-14:
              best_c=cur_c; best_h=h.copy()
       no_improve=0
       if steps > 20000: break
    # ensure best_h is projected and exact estimate? Return best_h, best_c raw.
    return best_h, best_c
```
Need update c_raw after applying `c_raw = c_raw + e*d` alloc; q loop:
```

```

```
def gaussian_blur1d(x, sigma):
   if sigma <=0: return x
   r=int(np.ceil(3*sigma))
   k=np.exp(-0.5*(np.arange(-r,r+1)/sigma)**2); k/=k.sum()
   out=np.convolve(x,k,mode='same')
   return out
```
No lambda. For sigma 1.5, kernel 3. Good.

### FFT correlation orientation correctness

Now, think of a potential **theoretical issue**: The objective is over all integer lags in discrete correlation, not continuous shifts. If we double n, we add lags at half the previous shifts; max could increase significantly if the continuous function has high correlation at those shifts not sampled. Evaluation computes with that n. So yes, we can exploit discretization. A high-resolution lift may have higher max due to new lags; optimization can lower. Good.

- In `run_slqp`, if constraints list created each call, `sum_jac` returns new ones; okay.
- In high-res, `base = upsample_h(h_opt, n_opt, n2)` but h_opt length maybe n_opt. If best_n different, h_opt length n_opt. Good. If h_opt is a start at n_opt. If global best has lower c at parent but h_opt worse, base from h_opt. Fine.

Let's write.

After writing, mentally lint.

Code:
```
import numpy as np

import time
...
```

`project_feasible`: In correction loop, if diff >0:
```
add = np.minimum(step, 1.0 - h[free])
```
If step is large, add some 0; after loop diff reduces. Good. If diff <0, `add = np.maximum(step, -h[free])` where step negative; e.g. step=-0.1, -h may be -0.05, maximum(-0.1,-0.05)=-0.05 saturates. Good.
Then `h = np.clip`; diff recompute. If free empty but diff, final proportional distribution. Good.

`raw_corr_fft`: `np.maximum(raw, 0.0, out=raw)` after slice. The slice is view, okay.

`weights_value`: if alpha <=0? Could return c.max? Not needed but safe:
```
if alpha <= 0: w=zeros; w[argmax]=1; return w,m
```

`gradient_from_weights`: If `w` all zero? weights_value always sum 1. active weights sum1. Top sum1. Good.

`sum_jac`: `return np.ones(n, dtype=float)`.

In `run_lbfgs_aug`, if `res` attribute x not finite: `if not np.all(np.isfinite(x_new))`. Good.
`options={'maxiter': maxit, 'ftol': 1e-20, 'gtol': 1e-12, 'maxfun': maxit * 8}`. maxfun maybe too low if line search; but okay.

In `run_slqp`, `options={'maxiter': maxit, 'ftol': 1e-14, 'maxls': 20}`. SLSQP has `factor`? okay.

`gaussian_blur1d`: If sigma=0: return x.copy. Use `denom = np.sqrt(2.0*np.pi*sigma*sigma)`? We normalize after, no need. Kernel:
```
r = max(1, int(np.ceil(3.0*sigma)))
k = np.exp(-0.5*(np.arange(-r,r+1)/sigma)**2)
k /= k.sum()
return np.convolve(x,k,mode='same')
```
For sigma very small 0.8, r=3. Good.

`D_var`: uses `h[::-1]` slice view. Good.

`pair_min`: `num = grid_num`; if `num < 2`: return. Use local `craw = c_raw`. `tmp = np.empty_like(craw)`. For each e, if k==0 val=cmax. Else:
```
np.multiply(dd, e, out=tmp)
tmp += craw
e2 = e*e
tmp[i0] += e2*v0 ...
val = float(tmp.max())
```
If q_idx contains same index due to unusual? not. For safety, if duplicate, adding twice q_val correct. But if duplicate with q_val? not.
Refine loop: if refine_num<2 skip. Use for `kk in range(refine_num)`.

`exact_pair_polish`: At end, if `h` not feasible, project. `final_raw = raw_corr_fft`. If final c < best_c set. Return best_h (projected? If h not best, best_h was projected earlier). Ensure best_h projected: when copied h after recompute, h feasible (maybe tiny). At final, if best_h not same? best_h copied from h after previous recompute; should be feasible. But if h got worse and best_h old, old projected. Good.
Inside, when `h=project_feasible(h,n)` after recompute, `h` new. Need update `c_raw` after. Good.
If no donors, `no_improve +=1; if no_improve > 15: break; continue`.
After a pair accepted, `no_improve=0`. Good.
If `periodic recompute` finds `cur` lower, update best. If `cur` is higher due to drift, best remains. But c_raw reset to recomputed (exact), which may be higher than previous c_raw and thus `current` baseline. Then next steps may need overcome; okay.

In run, after high-res, if `c2` not finite skip. Use `if np.isfinite(c2) and c2 < best_c`.

Potential time issue: `exact_c5` called many times for n=2000 maybe 8M. We call after each stage (few). okay.

Let's now produce final code. Ensure no missing indentation. Use `.item()`? Not.

```python
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time


def project_feasible(x, n):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.5)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)

    lo = -1.0
    hi = 1.0
    for _ in range(70):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        val = float(np.sum(np.clip(x + mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if val > target:
            hi = mid
        else:
            lo = mid

    tau = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    h = np.clip(x + tau, 0.0, 1.0)

    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    for _ in range(120):
        if abs(diff) < 1e-15:
            break
        free = np.where((h > 1e-13) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-13))[0]
        if free.size == 0:
            break
        step = diff / float(free.size)
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h[free]
            add = np.minimum(step, room)
        else:
            room = h[free]
            add = np.maximum(step, -room)
        h[free] += add
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
        diff = target - float(h.sum())

    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-15:
        free = np.where((h > 1e-14) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-14))[0]
        if free.size > 0:
            if diff > 0.0:
                room = 1.0 - h[free]
                if room.sum() > 0.0:
                    h[free] += diff * (room / room.sum())
            else:
                room = h[free]
                if room.sum() > 0.0:
                    h[free] += diff * (room / room.sum())
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    return h


def exact_c5(h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full") * dx
    return float(np.max(c))


def upsample_h(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    m = len(h)
    if m == n:
        return project_feasible(h, n)
    if m <= 1:
        return project_feasible(np.full(n, float(h[0]) if m else 0.5), n)
    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m - 1)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n - 1)
    out = np.interp(new, old, h)
    return project_feasible(out, n)


def prep_fft(n):
    need = 3 * int(n) - 2
    nfft = 1
    while nfft < need:
        nfft <<= 1
    ones = np.zeros(nfft, dtype=float)
    ones[:n] = 1.0
    rOnes = np.fft.rfft(ones)
    return nfft, rOnes


def raw_corr_fft(h, n, nfft, rOnes):
    rH = np.fft.rfft(h, nfft)
    rB = rOnes - rH
    rHr = np.fft.rfft(h[::-1], nfft)
    raw = np.fft.irfft(rHr * rB, nfft)
    raw = raw[:2 * int(n) - 1]
    np.maximum(raw, 0.0, out=raw)
    return raw


def weights_value(c, alpha):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or alpha <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w, m

    sh = alpha * (c - m)
    shmax = float(np.max(sh))
    if not np.isfinite(shmax) or shmax < -745.0:
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w, m

    sh = sh - shmax
    w = np.exp(sh)
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w, m

    w /= s
    val = m + (shmax + float(np.log(s))) / alpha
    return w, val


def gradient_from_weights(h, w, n, nfft, rH=None):
    n = int(n)
    if rH is None:
        rH = np.fft.rfft(h, nfft)
    rW = np.fft.rfft(w, nfft)
    rWr = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], nfft)

    conv1 = np.fft.irfft(rH * rWr, nfft)
    conv2 = np.fft.irfft(rH * rW, nfft)

    T = conv1[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    U = conv2[n - 1:2 * n - 1]

    P = np.empty(2 * n, dtype=float)
    P[0] = 0.0
    P[1:] = np.cumsum(w)

    j = np.arange(n)
    S = P[2 * n - 1 - j] - P[n - 1 - j]

    return S - T - U


def smooth_value(x, n, alpha, nfft, rOnes):
    raw = raw_corr_fft(x, n, nfft, rOnes)
    c = raw * (2.0 / float(n))
    _, val = weights_value(c, alpha)
    return val


def smooth_grad(x, n, alpha, nfft, rOnes):
    raw = raw_corr_fft(x, n, nfft, rOnes)
    c = raw * (2.0 / float(n))
    w, _ = weights_value(c, alpha)
    g = gradient_from_weights(x, w, n, nfft)
    return g * (2.0 / float(n))


def smooth_value_pen(x, n, alpha, nfft, rOnes, mu, lam):
    val = smooth_value(x, n, alpha, nfft, rOnes)
    s = float(x.sum()) - 0.5 * float(n)
    val += 0.5 * mu * s * s + lam * s
    return val


def smooth_grad_pen(x, n, alpha, nfft, rOnes, mu, lam):
    g = smooth_grad(x, n, alpha, nfft, rOnes)
    s = float(x.sum()) - 0.5 * float(n)
    g = g + (mu * s + lam)
    return g


def sum_const(x, n):
    return float(x.sum()) - 0.5 * float(n)


def sum_jac(x, n):
    return np.ones(int(n), dtype=float)


def run_lbfgs_aug(h0, n, deadline, alphas, mu_init, maxit, nfft, rOnes):
    n = int(n)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    h = project_feasible(h0, n)
    raw0 = raw_corr_fft(h, n, nfft, rOnes)
    c0 = float(raw0.max()) * dx
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c0

    mu = float(mu_init)
    lam = 0.0
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n

    for alpha in alphas:
        if time.time() > deadline - 5.0:
            break

        raw_cur = raw_corr_fft(h, n, nfft, rOnes)
        c_cur = float(raw_cur.max()) * dx

        try:
            res = minimize(
                smooth_value_pen,
                h,
                jac=smooth_grad_pen,
                args=(n, alpha, nfft, rOnes, mu, lam),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={
                    "maxiter": int(maxit),
                    "ftol": 1e-20,
                    "gtol": 1e-12,
                    "maxfun": int(maxit) * 8,
                },
            )
            x_new = res.x
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(x_new)):
                x_new = h
        except Exception:
            x_new = h

        h_new = project_feasible(x_new, n)
        raw_new = raw_corr_fft(h_new, n, nfft, rOnes)
        c_new = float(raw_new.max()) * dx

        if c_new < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_c = c_new
            best_h = h_new.copy()

        if c_new < c_cur + 0.02:
            h = h_new
        else:
            h = best_h

        diff = float(h.sum()) - 0.5 * float(n)
        lam -= mu * diff
        if lam > 1e3:
            lam = 1e3
        elif lam < -1e3:
            lam = -1e3

        if abs(diff) > 1e-4:
            mu = min(1e4, mu * 2.0)
        elif abs(diff) < 1e-6:
            mu = max(float(mu_init), mu * 0.5)

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    raw_b = raw_corr_fft(best_h, n, nfft, rOnes)
    return best_h, float(raw_b.max()) * dx


def run_slqp(h0, n, deadline, alphas, maxit, nfft, rOnes):
    n = int(n)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    h = project_feasible(h0, n)
    raw0 = raw_corr_fft(h, n, nfft, rOnes)
    c0 = float(raw0.max()) * dx
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c0

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    constraints = [
        {
            "type": "eq",
            "fun": sum_const,
            "jac": sum_jac,
            "args": (n,),
        }
    ]

    for alpha in alphas:
        if time.time() > deadline - 5.0:
            break
        try:
            res = minimize(
                smooth_value,
                h,
                jac=smooth_grad,
                args=(n, alpha, nfft, rOnes),
                method="SLSQP",
                bounds=bounds,
                constraints=constraints,
                options={
                    "maxiter": int(maxit),
                    "ftol": 1e-14,
                    "maxls": 20,
                },
            )
            x_new = res.x
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(x_new)):
                continue
        except Exception:
            continue

        h_new = project_feasible(x_new, n)
        raw_new = raw_corr_fft(h_new, n, nfft, rOnes)
        c_new = float(raw_new.max()) * dx

        if c_new < best_c - 1e-15:
            best_c = c_new
            best_h = h_new.copy()

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    raw_b = raw_corr_fft(best_h, n, nfft, rOnes)
    return best_h, float(raw_b.max()) * dx


def gaussian_blur1d(x, sigma):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    if sigma <= 0.0:
        return x.copy()
    r = max(1, int(np.ceil(3.0 * sigma)))
    t = np.arange(-r, r + 1, dtype=float)
    k = np.exp(-0.5 * (t / sigma) ** 2)
    k /= k.sum()
    return np.convolve(x, k, mode="same")


def D_var(h, x, n):
    n = int(n)
    x = int(x)
    D = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
    one_minus_h = 1.0 - h
    D[n - 1 - x:2 * n - 1 - x] += one_minus_h
    D[x:n + x] += -h[::-1]
    return D


def pair_min(c_raw, d, eps_bound, q_idx, q_val, cmax, grid_num=24, refine_num=12):
    if eps_bound <= 1e-14:
        return 0.0, float(cmax)

    craw = c_raw
    dd = d
    tmp = np.empty_like(craw)

    i0 = int(q_idx[0])
    i1 = int(q_idx[1])
    i2 = int(q_idx[2])
    v0 = float(q_val[0])
    v1 = float(q_val[1])
    v2 = float(q_val[2])

    best_e = 0.0
    best_f = float(cmax)
    gn = max(2, int(grid_num))

    for k in range(gn):
        e = eps_bound * float(k) / float(gn - 1)
        if k == 0:
            val = best_f
        else:
            np.multiply(dd, e, out=tmp)
            tmp += craw
            e2 = e * e
            tmp[i0] += e2 * v0
            tmp[i1] += e2 * v1
            tmp[i2] += e2 * v2
            val = float(tmp.max())
        if val < best_f - 1e-15:
            best_f = val
            best_e = e

    if 0.0 < best_e < eps_bound and refine_num >= 2:
        step = eps_bound / float(gn - 1)
        lo = best_e - step
        hi = best_e + step
        if lo < 0.0:
            lo = 0.0
        if hi > eps_bound:
            hi = eps_bound
        rn = max(2, int(refine_num))
        for k in range(rn):
            e = lo + (hi - lo) * float(k) / float(rn - 1)
            np.multiply(dd, e, out=tmp)
            tmp += craw
            e2 = e * e
            tmp[i0] += e2 * v0
            tmp[i1] += e2 * v1
            tmp[i2] += e2 * v2
            val = float(tmp.max())
            if val < best_f - 1e-15:
                best_f = val
                best_e = e

    return best_e, best_f


def exact_pair_polish(h0, n, deadline, nfft, rOnes):
    n = int(n)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    h = project_feasible(h0, n)
    c_raw = raw_corr_fft(h, n, nfft, rOnes)
    best_c = float(c_raw.max()) * dx
    best_h = h.copy()

    no_improve = 0
    steps = 0

    if n <= 700:
        K = 6
        top_k = 6
    elif n <= 1500:
        K = 5
        top_k = 6
    else:
        K = 4
        top_k = 4

    grid_num = 24
    refine_num = 12

    while time.time() < deadline - 1.0:
        cmax_raw = float(c_raw.max())
        cur_c = cmax_raw * dx
        if cur_c < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_c = cur_c
            best_h = h.copy()

        rH = np.fft.rfft(h, nfft)
        score_d = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        score_r = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

        active_tol_raw = 2.0
        active = c_raw >= (cmax_raw - active_tol_raw)
        if not active.any():
            active[int(np.argmax(c_raw))] = True
        w = np.zeros_like(c_raw)
        cnt = float(active.sum())
        w[active] = 1.0 / cnt
        g = gradient_from_weights(h, w, n, nfft, rH)

        mask = (g > 1e-10) & (h > 1e-10)
        if mask.any():
            score_d[mask] = g[mask] * h[mask]
        mask = (g < -1e-10) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-10)
        if mask.any():
            score_r[mask] = (-g[mask]) * (1.0 - h[mask])

        top_k = min(top_k, max(3, len(c_raw) // 2))
        if top_k > 1:
            idx = np.argpartition(c_raw, len(c_raw) - top_k)[-top_k:]
            w2 = np.zeros_like(c_raw)
            w2[idx] = 1.0 / float(top_k)
            g2 = gradient_from_weights(h, w2, n, nfft, rH)

            mask = (g2 > 1e-10) & (h > 1e-10)
            if mask.any():
                cand = g2[mask] * h[mask]
                score_d[mask] = np.maximum(score_d[mask], cand)
            mask = (g2 < -1e-10) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-10)
            if mask.any():
                cand = (-g2[mask]) * (1.0 - h[mask])
                score_r[mask] = np.maximum(score_r[mask], cand)

        bad = -1.0e30
        score_d = np.where(score_d > 1e-20, score_d, bad)
        score_r = np.where(score_r > 1e-20, score_r, bad)

        donor_all = np.argsort(-score_d)[:K]
        receiver_all = np.argsort(-score_r)[:K]

        dop = []
        for ii in donor_all:
            ii = int(ii)
            if h[ii] > 1e-10 and score_d[ii] > 1e-20:
                dop.append(ii)
        rop = []
        for jj in receiver_all:
            jj = int(jj)
            if h[jj] < 1.0 - 1e-10 and score_r[jj] > 1e-20:
                rop.append(jj)

        if len(dop) == 0 or len(rop) == 0:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 18:
                break
            continue

        Dcache = {}
        for ii in dop:
            Dcache[ii] = D_var(h, ii, n)
        for jj in rop:
            if jj not in Dcache:
                Dcache[jj] = D_var(h, jj, n)

        best_pair = None
        best_f = cmax_raw

        for ii in dop:
            Di = Dcache[ii]
            eps_i = float(h[ii])
            for jj in rop:
                if ii == jj:
                    continue
                eps_bound = min(eps_i, 1.0 - float(h[jj]))
                if eps_bound <= 1e-12:
                    continue
                Djj = Dcache[jj]
                dvec = Djj - Di
                q_idx = np.array(
                    [n - 1, n - 1 + jj - ii, n - 1 + ii - jj],
                    dtype=int,
                )
                q_val = np.array([-2.0, 1.0, 1.0], dtype=float)
                e, f = pair_min(c_raw, dvec, eps_bound, q_idx, q_val, cmax_raw,
                                grid_num, refine_num)
                if f < best_f - 1e-13:
                    best_f = f
                    best_pair = (ii, jj, float(e))

        if best_pair is None:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 22:
                break
            continue

        ii, jj, e = best_pair
        if e <= 1e-14:
            no_improve += 1
            continue

        Di = Dcache[ii]
        Djj = Dcache[jj]
        dvec = Djj - Di
        q_idx = np.array(
            [n - 1, n - 1 + jj - ii, n - 1 + ii - jj],
            dtype=int,
        )
        q_val = np.array([-2.0, 1.0, 1.0], dtype=float)

        c_raw = c_raw + e * dvec
        e2 = e * e
        for qq in range(3):
            c_raw[q_idx[qq]] += e2 * q_val[qq]

        h[ii] -= e
        h[jj] += e

        if h[ii] < 0.0:
            h[ii] = 0.0
        elif h[ii] > 1.0:
            h[ii] = 1.0
        if h[jj] < 0.0:
            h[jj] = 0.0
        elif h[jj] > 1.0:
            h[jj] = 1.0

        steps += 1
        no_improve = 0

        if steps % 5 == 0 or abs(float(h.sum()) - 0.5 * float(n)) > 1e-10:
            if abs(float(h.sum()) - 0.5 * float(n)) > 1e-10:
                h = project_feasible(h, n)
            c_raw = raw_corr_fft(h, n, nfft, rOnes)
            cur_c = float(c_raw.max()) * dx
            if cur_c < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_c = cur_c
                best_h = h.copy()

        if steps > 25000:
            break

    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    final_raw = raw_corr_fft(h, n, nfft, rOnes)
    final_c = float(final_raw.max()) * dx
    if final_c < best_c - 1e-15:
        best_c = final_c
        best_h = h.copy()

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    return best_h, best_c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 8.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        par = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception:
        par = np.full(500, 0.5, dtype=float)

    par = np.where(np.isfinite(par), par, 0.5)
    if len(par) == 0:
        par = np.full(500, 0.5, dtype=float)

    n_par = int(len(par))
    par = project_feasible(par, n_par)

    best_h = par.copy()
    best_n = n_par
    best_c = exact_c5(par, n_par)

    if n_par <= 800 and time.time() < deadline - 80:
        try:
            dl_par = min(t0 + 35.0, deadline - 80.0)
            nfft_p, rOnes_p = prep_fft(n_par)
            h_p, c_p_est = run_lbfgs_aug(
                par,
                n_par,
                dl_par,
                [30, 100, 500],
                10.0,
                45,
                nfft_p,
                rOnes_p,
            )
            c_p = exact_c5(h_p, n_par)
            if np.isfinite(c_p) and c_p < best_c - 1e-13:
                best_c = c_p
                best_h = h_p
                best_n = n_par
        except Exception:
            pass

    if best_n <= 1000:
        n_opt = 1000 if best_n < 1000 else best_n
    elif best_n <= 2000:
        n_opt = best_n
    else:
        n_opt = 2000

    n_opt = int(n_opt)

    if best_n == n_opt:
        h_lin = project_feasible(best_h, n_opt)
    else:
        h_lin = upsample_h(best_h, best_n, n_opt)

    starts = [h_lin]

    if best_n * 2 == n_opt and time.time() < deadline - 250:
        h_dup = np.empty(n_opt, dtype=float)
        h_dup[0::2] = best_h
        h_dup[1::2] = best_h
        h_dup = project_feasible(h_dup, n_opt)
        starts.append(h_dup)

    if time.time() < deadline - 250:
        for sg in (1.0, 2.0):
            hs = project_feasible(gaussian_blur1d(h_lin, sg), n_opt)
            starts.append(hs)
        noise = 0.05 * rng.normal(size=n_opt)
        noise = gaussian_blur1d(noise, 1.0)
        hn = project_feasible(h_lin + noise, n_opt)
        starts.append(hn)

    nfft, rOnes = prep_fft(n_opt)
    dx_opt = 2.0 / float(n_opt)

    h_opt = starts[0]
    h_opt_c = float(raw_corr_fft(h_opt, n_opt, nfft, rOnes).max()) * dx_opt
    for s in starts[1:]:
        c_s = float(raw_corr_fft(s, n_opt, nfft, rOnes).max()) * dx_opt
        if np.isfinite(c_s) and c_s < h_opt_c:
            h_opt_c = c_s
            h_opt = s

    if time.time() < deadline - 450:
        dl1 = min(t0 + 360.0, deadline - 450.0)
        if dl1 > time.time() + 10.0:
            if n_opt <= 1200:
                alphas1 = [10, 30, 100, 300, 1000, 3000]
                maxit1 = 80
            elif n_opt <= 1600:
                alphas1 = [10, 50, 200, 800, 3000]
                maxit1 = 60
            else:
                alphas1 = [10, 50, 200, 800]
                maxit1 = 45
            try:
                h_opt, c_est = run_lbfgs_aug(
                    h_opt, n_opt, dl1, alphas1, 10.0, maxit1, nfft, rOnes
                )
                c_req = exact_c5(h_opt, n_opt)
                if np.isfinite(c_req) and c_req < best_c - 1e-13:
                    best_c = c_req
                    best_h = h_opt
                    best_n = n_opt
            except Exception:
                pass

    if time.time() < deadline - 300:
        dl2 = min(t0 + 560.0, deadline - 300.0)
        if dl2 > time.time() + 10.0:
            if n_opt <= 1200:
                alphas2 = [3000, 10000, 30000]
                maxit2 = 35
            elif n_opt <= 1600:
                alphas2 = [5000, 20000]
                maxit2 = 30
            else:
                alphas2 = [8000, 30000]
                maxit2 = 22
            try:
                h_opt, c_est = run_slqp(h_opt, n_opt, dl2, alphas2, maxit2, nfft, rOnes)
                c_req = exact_c5(h_opt, n_opt)
                if np.isfinite(c_req) and c_req < best_c - 1e-13:
                    best_c = c_req
                    best_h = h_opt
                    best_n = n_opt
            except Exception:
                pass

    if time.time() < deadline - 40:
        dl3 = min(t0 + 1000.0, deadline - 40.0)
        if dl3 > time.time() + 30.0:
            try:
                h_opt, c_est = exact_pair_polish(h_opt, n_opt, dl3, nfft, rOnes)
                c_req = exact_c5(h_opt, n_opt)
                if np.isfinite(c_req) and c_req < best_c - 1e-13:
                    best_c = c_req
                    best_h = h_opt
                    best_n = n_opt
            except Exception:
                pass

    if time.time() < deadline - 90 and best_n == n_opt:
        try:
            noise = 0.045 * rng.normal(size=n_opt)
            noise = gaussian_blur1d(noise, 1.0)
            h_try = project_feasible(h_opt + noise, n_opt)
            c_try_est = float(raw_corr_fft(h_try, n_opt, nfft, rOnes).max()) * dx_opt
            base_corr = float(raw_corr_fft(h_opt, n_opt, nfft, rOnes).max()) * dx_opt
            if c_try_est < base_corr:
                h_opt = h_try

            dl4 = min(deadline - 60.0, time.time() + 70.0)
            if dl4 > time.time() + 10.0:
                h_try, _ = run_slqp(
                    h_opt,
                    n_opt,
                    dl4 - 15.0,
                    [20000, 50000],
                    22,
                    nfft,
                    rOnes,
                )
                c_try = exact_c5(h_try, n_opt)
                if np.isfinite(c_try) and c_try < best_c - 1e-13:
                    best_c = c_try
                    best_h = h_try
                    best_n = n_opt
                    h_opt = h_try

            if time.time() < deadline - 25:
                h_try, _ = exact_pair_polish(h_try, n_opt, deadline - 5.0, nfft, rOnes)
                c_try = exact_c5(h_try, n_opt)
                if np.isfinite(c_try) and c_try < best_c - 1e-13:
                    best_c = c_try
                    best_h = h_try
                    best_n = n_opt
        except Exception:
            pass

    if best_c > 0.38080 and time.time() < deadline - 250:
        try:
            n2 = 2000
            base2 = upsample_h(h_opt if len(h_opt) == n_opt else best_h, n_opt if len(h_opt) == n_opt else best_n, n2)
            nfft2, rOnes2 = prep_fft(n2)

            dl5 = min(time.time() + 170.0, deadline - 180.0)
            if dl5 > time.time() + 15.0:
                h2, _ = run_lbfgs_aug(
                    base2,
                    n2,
                    dl5,
                    [10, 50, 200, 800],
                    10.0,
                    50,
                    nfft2,
                    rOnes2,
                )
                c2 = exact_c5(h2, n2)
                if np.isfinite(c2) and c2 < best_c - 1e-13:
                    best_c = c2
                    best_h = h2
                    best_n = n2

            if best_n != n2 and time.time() < deadline - 90:
                dl6 = min(time.time() + 70.0, deadline - 90.0)
                if dl6 > time.time() + 10.0:
                    h2, _ = run_slqp(h2, n2, dl6, [20000], 20, nfft2, rOnes2)
                    c2 = exact_c5(h2, n2)
                    if np.isfinite(c2) and c2 < best_c - 1e-13:
                        best_c = c2
                        best_h = h2
                        best_n = n2
                    h2 = project_feasible(h2, n2)

            if time.time() < deadline - 25:
                base3 = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
                nft3, rO3 = prep_fft(best_n)
                h3, _ = exact_pair_polish(base3, best_n, deadline - 5.0, nft3, rO3)
                c3 = exact_c5(h3, best_n)
                if np.isfinite(c3) and c3 < best_c - 1e-13:
                    best_c = c3
                    best_h = h3
                    best_n = best_n
        except Exception:
            pass

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, int(best_n))
    best_c = exact_c5(best_h, int(best_n))
    if not np.isfinite(best_c):
        best_h = par.copy()
        best_n = n_par
        best_c = exact_c5(best_h, best_n)

    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```
